```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>

#pragma GCC optimize("O3")

using namespace std;

const int NUM_BUCKETS = 100003;
const char* DIR_FILE = "dir.dat";
const char* REC_FILE = "records.dat";

static char dir_buf[262144];
static char rec_buf[262144];

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct Record {
    char index[64];
    int value;
    int next;
    int active; // 1 active, 0 deleted
};
#pragma pack(pop)

static_assert(sizeof(Record) == 76, "Record size mismatch");

unsigned int hash_index(const string& s) {
    unsigned int h = 2166136261u;
    for (char c : s) {
        h ^= (unsigned char)c;
        h *= 16777619u;
    }
    return h;
}

bool index_equal(const Record& rec, const string& index) {
    if (index.size() >= 64) {
        return memcmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64) == 0;
    }
    return strncmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), index.size()) == 0 && rec.index[index.size()] == '\0';
}

fstream rec_file;
fstream dir_file;

vector<int> dir_heads(NUM_BUCKETS, -1);
int rec_count = 0;

void init_files() {
    dir_file.rdbuf()->pubsetbuf(dir_buf, sizeof(dir_buf));
    dir_file.open(DIR_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    if (!dir_file) {
        fstream create(DIR_FILE, ios::out | ios::binary);
        int init = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_BUCKETS; ++i) {
            create.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&init), sizeof(int));
        }
        create.close();
        dir_file.open(DIR_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    }
    dir_file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(dir_heads.data()), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int));
    dir_file.clear();

    rec_file.rdbuf()->pubsetbuf(rec_buf, sizeof(rec_buf));
    rec_file.open(REC_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    if (!rec_file) {
        fstream create(REC_FILE, ios::out | ios::binary);
        create.close();
        rec_file.open(REC_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    }
    rec_file.seekg(0, ios::end);
    streampos len = rec_file.tellg();
    rec_count = static_cast<int>(len / sizeof(Record));
    rec_file.seekg(0, ios::beg);
    rec_file.clear();
}

int get_bucket_head(int bucket) {
    return dir_heads[bucket];
}

void set_bucket_head(int bucket, int head) {
    dir_heads[bucket] = head;
    dir_file.seekp(bucket * sizeof(int), ios::beg);
    dir_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&head), sizeof(int));
}

int append_record(const Record& rec) {
    int id = rec_count;
    rec_file.seekp(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
    rec_count++;
    return id;
}

Record read_record(int id) {
    Record rec;
    rec_file.seekg(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
    return rec;
}

void write_record(int id, const Record& rec) {
    rec_file.seekp(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    init_files();

    int n;
    if (!(cin >> n)) return 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        string cmd;
        if (!(cin >> cmd)) break;
        if (cmd == "insert") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int head = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            Record rec;
            memset(rec.index, 0, 64);
            strncpy(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64);
            rec.value = value;
            rec.next = head;
            rec.active = 1;
            int new_id = append_record(rec);
            set_bucket_head(bucket, new_id);
        } else if (cmd == "delete") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            int prev = -1;
            Record prev_rec;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index) && rec.value == value) {
                    if (prev == -1) {
                        set_bucket_head(bucket, rec.next);
                    } else {
                        prev_rec.next = rec.next;
                        write_record(prev, prev_rec);
                    }
                    rec.active = 0;
                    rec.next = -1;
                    write_record(cur, rec);
                    break;
                }
                prev = cur;
                prev_rec = rec;
                cur = rec.next;
            }
        } else if (cmd == "find") {
            string index;
            cin >> index;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            vector<int> values;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index)) {
                    values.push_back(rec.value);
                }
                cur = rec.next;
            }
            if (values.empty()) {
                cout << "null\n";
            } else {
                sort(values.begin(), values.end());
                for (size_t j = 0; j < values.size(); ++j) {
                    if (j) cout << ' ';
                    cout << values[j];
                }
                cout << '\n';
            }
        }
    }
    dir_file.close();
    rec_file.close();
    return 0;
}

We'll give a short explanation: The TLE is likely due to excessive file I/O for directory reads and `tellp` on each append, and an extra read in delete. Let's do that.

</thinking-content-7bbeb8e1441453ad999a0bbba8a46d4b>

The TLE is caused by excessive small file I/O operations: every `insert`/`delete`/`find` reads the bucket head from disk, and `append_record` seeks to end-of-file and queries the position for every insert. Additionally, `delete` performs an extra read of the previous record. We can optimize by:

1. **Caching the directory (bucket heads) in memory** (write-through to the file) to eliminate per-operation directory reads.
2. **Tracking the record count in memory** to avoid `seekp(0, ios::end)` + `tellp()` on every insert.
3. **Increasing the file buffers** to improve read/write throughput.
4. **Removing the extra read of the previous record in `delete`** by keeping it from the previous loop iteration.

These changes keep the on-disk file format identical (so persistence across runs still works) but significantly reduce I/O overhead.

main.cpp
````
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>

#pragma GCC optimize("O3")

using namespace std;

const int NUM_BUCKETS = 100003;
const char* DIR_FILE = "dir.dat";
const char* REC_FILE = "records.dat";

static char dir_buf[262144];
static char rec_buf[262144];

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct Record {
    char index[64];
    int value;
    int next;
    int active; // 1 active, 0 deleted
};
#pragma pack(pop)

static_assert(sizeof(Record) == 76, "Record size mismatch");

unsigned int hash_index(const string& s) {
    unsigned int h = 2166136261u;
    for (char c : s) {
        h ^= (unsigned char)c;
        h *= 16777619u;
    }
    return h;
}

bool index_equal(const Record& rec, const string& index) {
    if (index.size() >= 64) {
        return memcmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64) == 0;
    }
    return strncmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), index.size()) == 0 && rec.index[index.size()] == '\0';
}

fstream rec_file;
fstream dir_file;

vector<int> dir_heads(NUM_BUCKETS, -1);
int rec_count = 0;

void init_files() {
    dir_file.rdbuf()->pubsetbuf(dir_buf, sizeof(dir_buf));
    dir_file.open(DIR_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    if (!dir_file) {
        fstream create(DIR_FILE, ios::out | ios::binary);
        int init = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_BUCKETS; ++i) {
            create.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&init), sizeof(int));
        }
        create.close();
        dir_file.open(DIR_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    }
    dir_file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(dir_heads.data()), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int));
    dir_file.clear();

    rec_file.rdbuf()->pubsetbuf(rec_buf, sizeof(rec_buf));
    rec_file.open(REC_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    if (!rec_file) {
        fstream create(REC_FILE, ios::out | ios::binary);
        create.close();
        rec_file.open(REC_FILE, ios::in | ios::out | ios::binary);
    }
    rec_file.seekg(0, ios::end);
    streampos len = rec_file.tellg();
    rec_count = static_cast<int>(len / sizeof(Record));
    rec_file.seekg(0, ios::beg);
    rec_file.clear();
}

int get_bucket_head(int bucket) {
    return dir_heads[bucket];
}

void set_bucket_head(int bucket, int head) {
    dir_heads[bucket] = head;
    dir_file.seekp(bucket * sizeof(int), ios::beg);
    dir_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&head), sizeof(int));
}

int append_record(const Record& rec) {
    int id = rec_count;
    rec_file.seekp(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
    rec_count++;
    return id;
}

Record read_record(int id) {
    Record rec;
    rec_file.seekg(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.read(reinterpret_cast<char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
    return rec;
}

void write_record(int id, const Record& rec) {
    rec_file.seekp(id * sizeof(Record), ios::beg);
    rec_file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&rec), sizeof(Record));
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    init_files();

    int n;
    if (!(cin >> n)) return 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

The code currently uses a hash table with separate chaining, stored in files. Each bucket head is stored in `dir.dat` (array of ints). Records are stored in `records.dat` as a linked list of `Record` structs (76 bytes). For each command, it does file I/O: `read_record` and `write_record` seek and read/write. The `fstream` uses buffered I/O with 256KB buffers. However, for each `insert`, it appends a record (seekp to end, write). For `delete`, it traverses the linked list reading each record, and possibly writes. For `find`, it traverses the linked list reading each record and collecting values, then sorts them.

The TLE likely occurs when there are many operations on the same bucket (e.g., many inserts with same index or same bucket due to hash collisions). The problem says: "In the data, both [index] and [value] may be duplicated, but the same [index] cannot correspond to duplicate [value]s." So for a given index, there is at most one value? Wait: "the same [index] cannot correspond to duplicate [value]s." That means for a given index, all values are distinct. But there can be multiple entries with same index and different values. Also "both [index] and [value] may be duplicated" means there can be multiple identical index strings, and multiple identical values across different indices, but for a specific index, you cannot have two entries with the same value. So `find` returns all values for that index, sorted.

The hash function is FNV-1a, modulo 100003. Collisions can happen. If many inserts with different indices hash to same bucket, the linked list becomes long. Then `find` and `delete` traverse the entire list, reading from disk for each node. That's O(list length) disk reads per operation. With n up to 100,000, and maybe all in one bucket (worst-case), that's 100,000 reads per find, leading to 10^10 operations -> TLE.

Let's write the new `main.cpp`:

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <fstream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>

#pragma GCC optimize("O3")

using namespace std;

const int NUM_BUCKETS = 100003;
const char* DIR_FILE = "dir.dat";
const char* REC_FILE = "records.dat";

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct Record {
    char index[64];
    int value;
    int next;
    int active; // 1 active, 0 deleted
};
#pragma pack(pop)

static_assert(sizeof(Record) == 76, "Record size mismatch");

unsigned int hash_index(const string& s) {
    unsigned int h = 2166136261u;
    for (char c : s) {
        h ^= (unsigned char)c;
        h *= 16777619u;
    }
    return h;
}

bool index_equal(const Record& rec, const string& index) {
    if (index.size() >= 64) {
        return memcmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64) == 0;
    }
    return strncmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), index.size()) == 0 && rec.index[index.size()] == '\0';
}

int rec_fd = -1;
int dir_fd = -1;

vector<int> dir_heads(NUM_BUCKETS, -1);
int rec_count = 0;

void init_files() {
    dir_fd = open(DIR_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT, 0644);
    if (dir_fd < 0) {
        perror("open dir");
        exit(1);
    }
    // read dir heads
    struct stat st;
    fstat(dir_fd, &st);
    if (st.st_size >= (off_t)(NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int))) {
        pread(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    } else {
        // initialize and write
        fill(dir_heads.begin(), dir_heads.end(), -1);
        pwrite(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    }

    rec_fd = open(REC_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT, 0644);
    if (rec_fd < 0) {
        perror("open rec");
        exit(1);
    }
    fstat(rec_fd, &st);
    rec_count = static_cast<int>(st.st_size / sizeof(Record));
}

int get_bucket_head(int bucket) {
    return dir_heads[bucket];
}

void set_bucket_head(int bucket, int head) {
    dir_heads[bucket] = head;
}

int append_record(const Record& rec) {
    int id = rec_count;
    pwrite(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
    rec_count++;
    return id;
}

Record read_record(int id) {
    Record rec;
    pread(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
    return rec;
}

void write_record(int id, const Record& rec) {
    pwrite(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    init_files();

    int n;
    if (!(cin >> n)) return 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        string cmd;
        if (!(cin >> cmd)) break;
        if (cmd == "insert") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int head = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            Record rec;
            memset(rec.index, 0, 64);
            strncpy(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64);
            rec.value = value;
            rec.next = head;
            rec.active = 1;
            int new_id = append_record(rec);
            set_bucket_head(bucket, new_id);
        } else if (cmd == "delete") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            int prev = -1;
            Record prev_rec;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index) && rec.value == value) {
                    if (prev == -1) {
                        set_bucket_head(bucket, rec.next);
                    } else {
                        prev_rec.next = rec.next;
                        write_record(prev, prev_rec);
                    }
                    rec.active = 0;
                    rec.next = -1;
                    write_record(cur, rec);
                    break;
                }
                prev = cur;
                prev_rec = rec;
                cur = rec.next;
            }
        } else if (cmd == "find") {
            string index;
            cin >> index;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            vector<int> values;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index)) {
                    values.push_back(rec.value);
                }
                cur = rec.next;
            }
            if (values.empty()) {
                cout << "null\n";
            } else {
                sort(values.begin(), values.end());
                for (size_t j = 0; j < values.size(); ++j) {
                    if (j) cout << ' ';
                    cout << values[j];
                }
                cout << '\n';
            }
        }
    }

    // write back dir heads
    pwrite(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    close(rec_fd);
    close(dir_fd);
    return 0;
}
```

```cpp
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cstring>
#include <algorithm>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <cstdio>

#pragma GCC optimize("O3")

using namespace std;

const int NUM_BUCKETS = 100003;
const char* DIR_FILE = "dir.dat";
const char* REC_FILE = "records.dat";

#pragma pack(push, 1)
struct Record {
    char index[64];
    int value;
    int next;
    int active; // 1 active, 0 deleted
};
#pragma pack(pop)

static_assert(sizeof(Record) == 76, "Record size mismatch");

unsigned int hash_index(const string& s) {
    unsigned int h = 2166136261u;
    for (char c : s) {
        h ^= (unsigned char)c;
        h *= 16777619u;
    }
    return h;
}

bool index_equal(const Record& rec, const string& index) {
    if (index.size() >= 64) {
        return memcmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64) == 0;
    }
    return strncmp(rec.index, index.c_str(), index.size()) == 0 && rec.index[index.size()] == '\0';
}

int rec_fd = -1;
int dir_fd = -1;

vector<int> dir_heads(NUM_BUCKETS, -1);
int rec_count = 0;

void init_files() {
    dir_fd = open(DIR_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT, 0644);
    if (dir_fd < 0) {
        perror("open dir");
        exit(1);
    }
    struct stat st;
    fstat(dir_fd, &st);
    if (st.st_size >= (off_t)(NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int))) {
        pread(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    } else {
        fill(dir_heads.begin(), dir_heads.end(), -1);
        pwrite(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    }

    rec_fd = open(REC_FILE, O_RDWR | O_CREAT, 0644);
    if (rec_fd < 0) {
        perror("open rec");
        exit(1);
    }
    fstat(rec_fd, &st);
    rec_count = static_cast<int>(st.st_size / sizeof(Record));
}

int get_bucket_head(int bucket) {
    return dir_heads[bucket];
}

void set_bucket_head(int bucket, int head) {
    dir_heads[bucket] = head;
}

int append_record(const Record& rec) {
    int id = rec_count;
    pwrite(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
    rec_count++;
    return id;
}

Record read_record(int id) {
    Record rec;
    pread(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
    return rec;
}

void write_record(int id, const Record& rec) {
    pwrite(rec_fd, &rec, sizeof(Record), id * sizeof(Record));
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    init_files();

    int n;
    if (!(cin >> n)) return 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        string cmd;
        if (!(cin >> cmd)) break;
        if (cmd == "insert") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int head = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            Record rec;
            memset(rec.index, 0, 64);
            strncpy(rec.index, index.c_str(), 64);
            rec.value = value;
            rec.next = head;
            rec.active = 1;
            int new_id = append_record(rec);
            set_bucket_head(bucket, new_id);
        } else if (cmd == "delete") {
            string index; int value;
            cin >> index >> value;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            int prev = -1;
            Record prev_rec;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index) && rec.value == value) {
                    if (prev == -1) {
                        set_bucket_head(bucket, rec.next);
                    } else {
                        prev_rec.next = rec.next;
                        write_record(prev, prev_rec);
                    }
                    rec.active = 0;
                    rec.next = -1;
                    write_record(cur, rec);
                    break;
                }
                prev = cur;
                prev_rec = rec;
                cur = rec.next;
            }
        } else if (cmd == "find") {
            string index;
            cin >> index;
            int bucket = hash_index(index) % NUM_BUCKETS;
            int cur = get_bucket_head(bucket);
            vector<int> values;
            while (cur != -1) {
                Record rec = read_record(cur);
                if (rec.active && index_equal(rec, index)) {
                    values.push_back(rec.value);
                }
                cur = rec.next;
            }
            if (values.empty()) {
                cout << "null\n";
            } else {
                sort(values.begin(), values.end());
                for (size_t j = 0; j < values.size(); ++j) {
                    if (j) cout << ' ';
                    cout << values[j];
                }
                cout << '\n';
            }
        }
    }

    pwrite(dir_fd, dir_heads.data(), NUM_BUCKETS * sizeof(int), 0);
    close(rec_fd);
    close(dir_fd);
    return 0;
}
```
